Problem minimize maximum coefficient in product A(z)A(z) with nonnegative A coefficients sum 1. This is equivalent to finding a probability measure μ on [0,n-1] minimizing the maximum mass of μ+μ. This is "maximal component of sumset". There is known "Ruzsa's inequality" and "giant component" for sets. For continuous, optimal distribution might be a "tent" or "double exponential". There is "Bernstein's problem": find polynomial with nonnegative coefficients whose square has minimal sup norm on interval? Maybe related to "Bohr's problem".

Now exact/local optimization details.

Let's design optimizer `optimize_seq(a_init, n, seconds, seed, target_score=None)`:
- Input normalized p positive.
- Use log v = log(p), then mean zero; but if p has zeros, log 0 -> large negative. Use floor 1e-12.
- Precompute m=next_pow2(2n-1).
- Keep best p and score exact (FFT). Evaluate every few it.
- Maintain Adam moments.
- Iterate until deadline or stagnant.
- At each iteration:
   p = softmax(v) stable. To fix exact sum=1 (already). But if v clipped, sum normalized anyway.
   F = fft(p,m)
   b = ifft(F*F).real[:2n-1]; b = max(b,0)
   bmax = max(b)
   Compute weights:
      base tau = max(1e-6, bmax * (0.02 + 0.20*stuck_factor?))
      We can choose tau fractional schedule decreasing to e.g. 0.02. Maybe with restart increasing.
      w = softmax((b - bmax)/tau)

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_seq(a_init, n, seconds, seed):
   p=normalize(a_init)
   v=np.log(p+1e-300); v-=mean
   best=p.copy(); best_sc=score from b
   mu=nu=zeros; t=0
   lr=0.25; min_lr=0.02; max_lr=0.6
   tau_frac=0.15
   K_frac=0.10; boost=0.5
   start=monotonic; deadline=start+seconds; last_improve=start
   it=0
   while time<deadline:
       progress=(time-start)/seconds
       p=softmax(v)
       F=fft(p,m)
       b=ifft(F*F).real[:L]
       b=np.maximum(b,0)
       bmax=b.max()
       sc=2*n*bmax  # since sum1
       if sc<best_sc - 1e-10: update best,last_improve
       # if stuck: perturb lr, noise, or blend
       if time-last_improve > 30:
           # increase temperature/K, or noise
           if rng.random()<0.3: v += N(0, 0.2)
           lr = max(min_lr, lr*0.6)
           tau_frac = min(0.5, tau_frac*1.5)
           K_frac = min(0.5, K_frac*1.5)
           last_improve = monotonic? careful resets too often
       # weight
       tau=max(1e-12, bmax*(tau_frac*(1-0.5*progress)+0.01))
       x=(b-bmax)/tau; w=exp(clip(x,-60,0)); w/=sum
       K=max(1,int(L*K_frac)); if K<L: idx=argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]; w[idx]+=boost*(1-progress)+0.1; w/=sum
       Fw=fft(w,m)
       c=ifft(Fw*conj(F)).real[:n]
       grad=p*(c-dot(p,c)); grad/=np.sqrt(mean(grad^2)+1e-18)
       # if nan/skip
       t+=1; Adam update
       step=clip(...)
       v -= lr*step; mean zero; clip (-40,40)
   final score, return best normalized, best_sc
```

- Projection simplex for K: standard:
```
def proj_simplex(x):
   n=x.size
   u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
   cssv=np.cumsum(u)
   ind=np.arange(1,n+1)
   cond=u - (cssv-1)/ind > 0
   rho=np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]
   theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
   return np.maximum(x-theta,0)
```
But x may not sum1; operation x-theta. Good.
- If K large, sort cheap.

Let's outline `optimize_seq` with QP:
```
def optimize_seq(a, n, seconds, seed, ambient_score=None):
    p=normalize(a); v=log(p)
    m=next_pow2(2n-1); L=2n-1
    best=p; best_sc=score; last_improve=now
    K = int(min(L, max(16, min(96, 0.05*L))))
    w_update=3
    q_iter=0
    lr=0.3; base...
    while time<deadline:
        progress=...
        p=soft(v)
        F=fft(p,m); b=ifft(F*F).real[:L]; b=max(0)
        bmax=b.max(); sc=2n*bmax
        if sc<best_sc: update best, last_improve
        # choose active A
        if q_iter % max(1, K//8?) ==0 or stuck:
           A=top_k_indices(b,k)
           H, G = compute_h_and_gram(p,b,A)
           wv=qp_weights(G, K)
           Hw = H.T.dot(wv)  # v-gradient
           # residual = norm(Hw)
           # if residual/small or no QP, fallback soft
        # soft weights for fallback/blend
        tau=...
        w_soft=softmax(active)
        # blend: if q_update just did, use Hw; else use H @ wv (stale) maybe recompute Hw. If QP residual > threshold, use soft.
        grad = Hw if use_q else soft_grad (compute via FFT or direct)
        # normalize, Adam, update v
        q_iter+=1
```

Implementation:
```
def build_H(p,b,A):
    n=len(p); K=len(A); H=np.empty((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r,k in enumerate(A):
        row=H[r]
        row.fill(0.0)
        l0 = k - (n-1)
        if l0 < 0: l0=0
        l1 = k
        if l1 > n-1: l1=n-1
        if l1 >= l0:
            l_idx = np.arange(l0,l1+1)
            j_idx = k - l_idx
            row[j_idx] = p[l_idx]
        row -= b[k]
        row *= p
    return H
```
This allocates l_idx each row. Could precompute arange once? `np.arange(l0,l1+1)` length up to n, maybe heavy. But K small. Use vectorized assignments.
`row.fill(0)` each row; H allocated uninitialized.
Potential memory: H K*n for K=48,n=8192=4MB; fine. For H@H.T, use `H @ H.T`.

def qp_weights(G, iters=None):
    K=G.shape[0]
    if K==1: return np.ones(1)
    G=0.5*(G+G.T); G += 1e-12*np.eye(K)?
    # normalize? trace
    # eigen
    try: eig=np.linalg.eigvalsh(G); Lmax=max(1e-12, eig[-1])
    except: Lmax=2*np.max(np.abs(np.sum(G,axis=1)))
    w=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    step=2.0/Lmax? For f=w^T G w, grad=2Gw, Lipschitz 2Lmax, step=1/Lmax.
    for it in range(iters or 80):
       g=G.dot(w)
       if not np.all... break
       wnew=proj_simplex(w - (2.0/Lmax)*g?) Actually grad=2Gw, step alpha. Choose alpha=1/Lmax => w - alpha*2Gw = w - 2Gw/Lmax. Or use step=1/(2*Lmax) with grad=2? Let's just use w = proj(w - (1.0/Lmax)*g) since optimizing w^TG w? gradient of w^T G w is 2Gw; using alpha=1/Lmax gives w-2Gw/Lmax. Good enough. For PSD, converges.
       delta=np.linalg.norm(wnew-w,1)
       w=wnew
       if delta<1e-9: break
    # normalize
    s=w.sum(); if s>0 w/=s else uniform
    return w
```

Let's write `score_fft` carefully:
```
def score_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return inf
    a=a/s
    m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
    b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    bert=b.max()
    if not finite or bert<=0: # if all zero? invalid
    return 2.0*n*bert
```
Since we normalize inside. For speed, if caller already normalized, avoid re-normal? Could have `_score_normalized(a)`. But okay.

Let's think about the active-set QP more carefully. If we solve QP weights with H rows only top K, and use grad = H.T@w each iteration, this is equivalent to gradient descent on a particular weighted sum of active b's chosen to minimize gradient norm. But optimizing a weighted sum can increase max if weights not adapt; we recompute each iter. It might be too "stationary" and not descend objective strongly. We can instead use a **balanced gradient**: weights proportional to b (soft) but QP chooses optimal combination among active. QP's gradient norm may be very small near a point where active gradients positive hull contains zero even if objective can be reduced by moving inactive? For max objective, if active hull contains zero, active constraints cannot all be decreased along a common direction; any descent must increase at least one active. But max objective is at a local optimum if inactive gradients ≤ λ? Wait for min t, KKT with active multipliers. In max formulation, if subgradient 0 in convex hull of active gradients, a local min? For minimizing max of smooth functions, if there is a subgradient with zero? The subdifferential at max is convex hull of active gradients. F is not differentiable; 0 in subdifferential implies local minimum (for convex; for nonconvex not sufficient). Here functions nonconvex, but likely needed. If 0 in hull, active-set gradient zero, p stationary for active sum but maybe local max? Could be. To leave, perturb.

If QP residual small, local optimum for active set. To improve, need include inactive or perturb. Our stuck logic will perturb.

Let's write `optimize_seq` pseudocode in detail:
```
def optimize_seq(a_init, n, seconds, seed):
    n=int(max(3,n)); seconds=max(1,seconds)
    p=normalise(a_init)
    v=np.log(p+1e-300); v-=np.mean(v); v=np.clip(v,-50,50)
    m=next_pow2(2*n-1); L=2*n-1
    p=soft(v)
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=score from p directly using helper maybe `_score_normalized(p,m)`
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    mu=zeros; nu=zeros; t=0
    base_lr=0.45; lr=base_lr
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    Kmax = ...
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+seconds
    last_improve=start
    stuck_since=start
    soft_mode=False; soft_left=0
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       now=time.monotonic(); if now>=deadline break
       progress=min(1,(now-start)/max(1e-9,seconds))
       # stuck detection
       if not soft_mode and now - last_improve > 45.0:
           # perturb
           sigma=0.05+0.20*(1-progress)
           v += rng.normal(0, sigma, n)
           v = v - mean; clip
           soft_mode = True; soft_left=30
           stuck_since = now
           # no reset last_improve? maybe set to now to avoid immediate? But last_improve tracks score; if we reset we may never know. Use separate `stuck_handled` time.
       if soft_left>0:
          soft_left-=1
          if soft_left==0: soft_mode=False
       # compute p,F,b
       p=soft(v)
       F=fft(p,m)
       b=ifft(F*F).real[:L]
       b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
       bmax=b.max()
       if not finite: break
       s=p.sum()
       sc=2*n*bmax/(s*s)  # s~1
       if sc<best_sc-1e-10:
           best_sc=sc; best=p.copy(); last_improve=monotonic()
       # Iters until deadline maybe expensive; if it%?? okay
       if not soft_mode:
          K = int(max(8, min(Kmax, Kmax*(0.5 + 0.5*progress))))
          K = min(K,L)
          A = top K b
          H = build_H(p,b,A)
          H -= np.mean(H, axis=1, keepdims=True)
          # remove rows with tiny norm
          row_norms = np.linalg.norm(H, axis=1); if any zero, K reduce
          G = H @ H.T
          G = 0.5*(G+G.T)
          # small ridge
          if size>1: G += 1e-12 * np.eye(K)
          wv=qp_weights(G, iters=60)
          grad = H.T @ wv
          grad_rms = sqrt(mean(grad*grad))
          if not finite or grad_rms<1e-12:
              soft_mode=True; soft_left=10
          else:
              # maybe occasionally mix soft if progress high
              if it % 60 == 59:
                  soft_limit? We'll handle by setting grad = 0.7*grad + 0.3*compute_soft? cost.
              else:
                  grad_normalized=grad/grad_rms
       if soft_mode:
           grad = compute_soft_grad(p,F,b,bmax,tau...,m,n)
           grad_rms=...
       if grad invalid: break or noise
       # Adam update
       gv=normalize? We already normalized; for Adam maybe use normalized? Previous normalized then Adam. Adam on normalized gradient with lr.
       gv = grad/grad_rms
       gv = np.clip(gv, -5,5) maybe
       t+=1; mu=...; nu=...
       mhat=mu/(1-b1)
       vhat=nu/(1-b2)
       lr = base_lr * (1.0 - 0.55*progress)
       step = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps); step=clip(-0.35,0.35)
       v = v - lr*step; v -= mean; clip(-40,40)
       it+=1
    # final evaluate p
    p=soft(v); sc=score; if sc<best... 
    return best normalized, best_sc
```

```
def _proj_simplex(x, size=1):
    n=x.size
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u - (cssv - size)/np.arange(1,n+1) > 0
    if not np.any(cond): return np.full(n,size/n)
    rho=np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-size)/(rho+1)
    y=x-theta
    np.maximum(y,0,out=y)
    s=y.sum()
    if s>0: y/=s
    else: y.fill(size/n)
    return y
```
This projects to sum=size. For size=1.

def _qp_solver(G, iters=60):

       eig=np.linalg.eigvalsh(G)

       Lmax=1e-14

def _soft_gradient(p,F,b,bmax,tau,m,n):
    x=(b-bmax)/max(1e-300,tau)
    x=np.clip(x,-70,0)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w=np.full(L,1/L)
    else w/=s
    # top boost maybe Ksoft = max(1,int(0.05*n))? maybe n? use 0.05 L
    K = max(1,int(0.05*b.size))
    if K < b.size:
       idx=np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]
       w[idx] += 0.5
       w/=w.sum()
    Fw=np.fft.fft(w,m)
    c=np.fft.ifft(Fw*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
    cm=float(np.dot(p,c))
    g=p*(c-cm)
    g-=np.mean(g) # optional? Common shift irrelevant. But p-weight? maybe.
    return g
```

"""

Uses FFT scoring, parametric starts (exponential, power, polynomial, cosine),

and local optimization across increasing lengths.

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    p=1
    while p<x: p <<=1
    return p
```

`_norm_seq`:
```
def _norm_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(3)/3
    np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0, copy=False)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
        return np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size)
    return a/s
```
No posinf? clamp. If all zero? uniform.

`_score_with_fft`:
```
def _score_with_fft(a, m=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<1e-300: return inf
    if m is None: m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    p=a/s
    F=np.fft.fft(p,m)
    b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    mx=float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx<=0: return inf
    return float(2.0*n*mx)
```

`_proj_simplex`:
Use `size` keyword? No lambdas.
```
def _proj_simplex(x, total=1.0):
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return x
    u=np.sort(x, kind='mergesort')[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    cond=(u - (cssv-total)/idx) > 0.0
    if not np.any(cond):
       return np.full(n,total/n)
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(cssv[rho]-total)/(rho+1)
    y=x-theta
    np.maximum(y,0.0,out=y)
    sy=float(y.sum())
    if sy<=0 or not np.isfinite(sy): return np.full(n,total/n)
    y/=sy
    return y
```

`_qp_solver`:
```
def _qp_solver(G, iters=70):
    K=G.shape[0]
    if K==0: return np.array([])
    if K==1: return np.array([1.0])
    G=np.array(G,dtype=np.float64)
    G=0.5*(G+G.T)
    tr=float(np.trace(G))
    if not np.isfinite(tr): return np.full(K,1.0/K)
    if tr <= 1e-15:
       return np.full(K,1.0/K)
    G += 1e-12 * (tr/K) * np.eye(K)
    try:
       ev=np.linalg.eigvalsh(G)
       Lm=float(ev[-1])
    except Exception:
       Lm=float(np.max(np.abs(np.sum(G, axis=1))))*K
    if not np.isfinite(Lm) or Lm<=1e-300: Lm=1.0
    step=2.0/Lm
    w=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    last=0.0
    for it in range(iters):
       g=G.dot(w)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break
       wnew=_proj_simplex(w - step*g,1.0)
       delta=float(np.sum(np.abs(wnew-w)))
       w=wnew
       if delta < 1e-10:
           break
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
Potential step too large if Lm estimate? Since G PSD with Lm largest eigen, step=2/Lm standard for gradient descent on f=w^T G w? Wait gradient=2Gw, Lipschitz=2Lm, ideal step=1/Lm. Multiplying weight `w - (1/Lm) * 2Gw = w - (2/Lm)g`, yes step=2/Lm. Good. Projection can oscillate but fine.

`_topk_indices`:
```
def _topk_indices(b,k):
    L=b.size
    k=int(max(1,min(k,L)))
    if k==L: return np.arange(L)
    idx=np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
    return idx
```

`_build_H`:
```
def _build_H(p,b,A):
    p=np.asarray(p); n=p.size
    K=A.size
    H=np.zeros((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    if K==0: return H
    for r in range(K):
       k=int(A[r])
       row=H[r]
       l0=k-(n-1)
       if l0<0: l0=0
       l1=k
       if l1>n-1: l1=n-1
       if l1>=l0:
          js=k-l1
          je=k-l0
          row[js:je+1]=p[l0:l1+1][::-1]
       m=float(b[k]) if k<b.size else 0.0
       row -= m
       row *= p
    return H
```
Need if b size >=L. Good.

`_soft_gradient`:
```
def _soft_gradient(p,F,b,bmax,tau,m,n):
    if bmax <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    tau=max(1e-300,bmax*tau)
    x=(b-bmax)/tau
    x=np.clip(x,-70.0,0.0)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       w=np.full(b.size,1.0/b.size)
    else: w/=s
    # top boost
    K=int(max(1, b.size*0.05))
    if K<b.size:
       idx=np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]
       w[idx]+=0.5
       s2=float(w.sum()); w/=s2
    Fw=np.fft.fft(w,m)
    c=np.fft.ifft(Fw*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
    cm=float(np.dot(p,c))
    g=p*(c-cm)
    g-=float(np.mean(g))
    return g
```
This recomputes Fw each call. Called occasionally.

`_interpolate`:
```
def _interpolate(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0); s=a.sum()
    n=int(max(1,n))
    if a.size==0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    a=a/s
    if a.size==n: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    s2=out.sum()
    if s2<=0: out.fill(1.0/n)
    else: out/=s2
    return out
```

Generation functions:
Use `x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) +0.5)/float(n)` maybe for density. For exponential linear, use x uniform? Continuous f(x)=exp(λ x) over [0,1]. With grid x=(i+0.5)/n. logp=λ*x. Subtract max for stability: `logp -= logp.max()` then exp. Normalize.
```
def _gen_exp(n,lam):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    lp=lam*x
    lp-=lp.max()
    p=np.exp(np.clip(lp,-700,700))
    p=np.maximum(p,0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p=np.ones(n)
    return p/s
```

def _gen_power(n,a,b):

    lp-=max

def _gen_poly(n,a,b):

    p=np.maximum(p,1e-6)
    s=p.sum(); return p/s

def _gen_cos(n,rng):

def _diverse_top(scored,k,thr=0.12):
    if not scored: return []
    order=sorted(scored, key=_score_key)
    chosen=[]; chseq=[]
    for sc,a in order:
       if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
       ok=True
       for ca in chseq:
          denom=float(np.linalg.norm(a)+np.linalg.norm(ca)+1e-300)
          if denom<=0: ok=False; break
          dist=float(np.linalg.norm(a-ca))/denom
          if dist<thr: ok=False; break
       if ok:
          chosen.append((sc,a)); chseq.append(a)
          if len(chosen)>=k: break
    # if fewer than k, fill with remaining best
    if len(chosen)<k:
       have=set(id(a) for a in chseq) # but sequences reusable? 
       for sc,a in order:
          if len(chosen)>=k: break
          if any(np.shares_memory? no)
```

```
def _optimize_seq(a_init,n,seconds,seed):
    n=int(max(3,n)); seconds=max(5.0,float(seconds))
    a0=_norm_seq(a_init)

              grad -= np.mean(grad)
              grms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad)))
              if not finite or grms<1e-14:
                  soft_active=True; soft_iters=10

`_soft` function:

def _soft(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    v=v-float(np.max(v))
    v=np.clip(v,-745,700)
    e=np.exp(v)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return full(1/n)
    return e/s
```

Let's implement `_active_weighted_gradient(p,b,A,weights)`:
- Build H for A? We already have `_build_H(p,b,A)` returns H rows. Then grad=H.T@weights; center. Weighted sum of v-gradients for selected lags. This works for any A and weights summing maybe not 1. A=_topk_indices(b,K)

def _top_weight_gradient(p,b,tau,K):
    L=b.size; n=p.size
    K=min(K,L)
    A=_topk_indices(b,K)
    bA=b[A]
    x=bA - float(bA.max()) # bmax over A approximately global bmax for top K
    x/=max(1e-300,tau)
    x=np.clip(x,-70,0)
    w=np.exp(x); s=w.sum(); if s>0 w/=s
    H=_build_H(p,b,A)
    H-=mean rows
    Hn=np.linalg.norm(H,axis=1)
    good=Hn>1e-12
    if not all: H=H[good]; w=w[good]; if sum invalid uniform
    g=H.T.dot(w)
    g-=mean(g)
    return g
```

```python
"""
Searches for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation combines:
  - FFT-based scoring,
  - many analytic parametric starts (exponentials, powers, quadratics,
    mixtures, low-order polynomials, small cosine perturbations),
  - interpolation of the previous best construction if available,
  - a log-softmax optimizer using an active-set QP gradient:
      for the hardest convolution lags it chooses weights that minimize
      the norm of the combined gradient, with periodic broader top-weight
      soft gradients and stochastic restarts.

The routine is time limited, keeps track of the best sequence seen, and
returns the scaled best valid sequence found.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _norm_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(3, dtype=np.float64) / 3.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0, copy=False)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _score_with_fft(a, m=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    if m is None:
        m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    p = a / s
    F = np.fft.fft(p, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    mx = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def _soft(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    n = v.size
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _score_key(x):
    return x[0]


def _proj_simplex(x, total=1.0):
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    u = np.sort(x, kind="mergesort")[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u - (cssv - total) / idx) > 0.0
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, total / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - total) / float(rho + 1)
    y = x - theta
    np.maximum(y, 0.0, out=y)
    sy = float(y.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sy) or sy <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, total / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    y /= sy
    return y


def _qp_solver(G, iters=60):
    G = np.asarray(G, dtype=np.float64)
    K = G.shape[0]
    if K == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    G = 0.5 * (G + G.T)
    tr = float(np.trace(G))
    if not np.isfinite(tr) or tr <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
    G = G + (1e-12 * (tr / float(K))) * np.eye(K, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        ev = np.linalg.eigvalsh(G)
        Lm = float(ev[-1])
    except Exception:
        Lm = float(np.max(np.abs(np.sum(G, axis=1)))) * float(K)
    if not np.isfinite(Lm) or Lm <= 1e-300:
        Lm = 1.0
    step = 2.0 / Lm
    w = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        g = G.dot(w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        wnew = _proj_simplex(w - step * g, 1.0)
        delta = float(np.sum(np.abs(wnew - w)))
        w = wnew
        if delta < 1e-10:
            break
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def _topk_indices(b, k):
    L = b.size
    k = int(max(1, min(k, L)))
    if k == L:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    return np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]


def _build_H(p, b, A):
    """Build rows of the log-softmax gradient for selected lags."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    K = int(A.size)
    H = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 0:
        return H
    for r in range(K):
        k = int(A[r])
        row = H[r]
        l0 = k - (n - 1)
        if l0 < 0:
            l0 = 0
        l1 = k
        if l1 > n - 1:
            l1 = n - 1
        if l1 >= l0:
            js = k - l1
            je = k - l0
            row[js:je + 1] = p[l0:l1 + 1][::-1]
        m = float(np.dot(p, row))
        row -= m
        row *= p
    return H


def _top_weight_gradient(p, b, bmax, tau, K):
    """Gradient from a softmax weighting of the top K lags."""
    L = b.size
    n = p.size
    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    A = _topk_indices(b, K)
    bA = b[A]
    tau = max(1e-300, float(tau))
    x = (bA - bmax) / tau
    x = np.clip(x, -70.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / s
    H = _build_H(p, b, A)
    H -= np.mean(H, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    rn = np.linalg.norm(H, axis=1)
    good = rn > 1e-13
    if not np.all(good):
        H = H[good, :]
        w = w[good]
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
            w = np.full(max(1, H.shape[0]), 1.0 / max(1, H.shape[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / wsum
        if H.shape[0] == 0:
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    g = H.T.dot(w)
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return g


def _interpolate(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = a / s
    if a.size == n:
        return a
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s2 = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        out.fill(1.0 / float(n))
    else:
        out /= s2
    return out


def _gen_exp(n, lam):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    lp = lam * x
    lp -= float(np.max(lp))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(lp, -700.0, 700.0))
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _gen_mixexp(n, lam, w):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = w * np.exp(lam * x) + (1.0 - w) * np.exp(-lam * x)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _gen_power(n, a, b):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    xr = 1.0 - x
    lp = a * np.log(x) + b * np.log(xr)
    lp -= float(np.max(lp))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(lp, -700.0, 700.0))
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _gen_quad(n, alpha, beta):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    lp = alpha * x + beta * (x - 0.5) ** 2
    lp -= float(np.max(lp))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(lp, -700.0, 700.0))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _gen_poly(n, a, b):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = 1.0 + a * x + b * x * x
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-6)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _gen_cos(n, rng):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    kc = int(max(2, min(7, n // 12 + 1)))
    for k in range(1, kc + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.55)) / (float(k) ** 0.7)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
        p += 0.35 * amp * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-6)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _initial_candidates(n, rng, prev):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    c = []
    if prev is not None:
        c.append(_interpolate(prev, n))
    c.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    for lam in (-4.0, -3.0, -2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0):
        c.append(_gen_exp(n, lam))

    for lam in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        for w in (0.15, 0.5, 0.85):
            c.append(_gen_mixexp(n, lam, w))

    powers = (-1.0, -0.5, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
    for a in powers:
        for b in powers:
            c.append(_gen_power(n, a, b))

    for al in (-3.0, -1.5, 0.0, 1.5, 3.0):
        for be in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0):
            c.append(_gen_quad(n, al, be))

    poly_vals = (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5)
    for a in poly_vals:
        for b in poly_vals:
            c.append(_gen_poly(n, a, b))

    for _ in range(8):
        c.append(_gen_cos(n, rng))

    return c


def _diverse_top(scored, k, thr=0.12):
    if not scored:
        return []
    order = sorted(scored, key=_score_key)
    chosen = []
    chseq = []
    for sc, a in order:
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        ok = True
        for ca in chseq:
            na = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
            nc = float(np.linalg.norm(ca))
            den = na + nc + 1e-300
            if den <= 0.0:
                ok = False
                break
            dist = float(np.linalg.norm(a - ca)) / den
            if dist < thr:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            chosen.append((sc, a))
            chseq.append(a)
            if len(chosen) >= k:
                break
    if len(chosen) < k:
        for sc, a in order:
            if len(chosen) >= k:
                break
            already = False
            for ca in chseq:
                if a is ca:
                    already = True
                    break
            if not already:
                chosen.append((sc, a))
                chseq.append(a)
    return chosen


def _optimize_seq(a_init, n, seconds, seed):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    seconds = max(10.0, float(seconds))
    a0 = _norm_seq(a_init)
    v = np.log(a0 + 1e-300)
    v -= float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
    p = _soft(v)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    F = np.fft.fft(p, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bmax = float(b.max())
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * bmax)
    best = p.copy()
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc) or best_sc <= 0.0:
        best = _norm_seq(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        v = np.log(best + 1e-300)
        best_sc = float(2.0 * n)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0

    if n <= 512:
        Kmax = 20
    elif n <= 1024:
        Kmax = 28
    elif n <= 4096:
        Kmax = 36
    elif n <= 8192:
        Kmax = 40
    else:
        Kmax = 36
    Ksoft = min(L, max(128, 6 * Kmax))

    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    base_lr = 0.45

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + seconds
    last_improve = start
    last_perturb = start
    soft_iters = 0
    it = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        elapsed = max(1e-9, now - start)
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / seconds)

        if soft_iters <= 0 and now - last_improve > 40.0 and now - last_perturb > 20.0:
            sigma = 0.04 + 0.22 * (1.0 - progress)
            v += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v -= float(np.mean(v))
            v = np.clip(v, -35.0, 35.0)
            soft_iters = 18
            last_perturb = now

        if soft_iters > 0:
            soft_iters -= 1

        p = _soft(v)
        F = np.fft.fft(p, m)
        b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        bmax = float(b.max())
        S = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * bmax / (S * S))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
            best_sc = sc
            best = p.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()

        grad = None
        grms = 0.0

        if soft_iters > 0:
            tau_frac = 0.08 + 0.18 * (1.0 - progress)
            g = _top_weight_gradient(p, b, bmax, max(1e-300, bmax * tau_frac), Ksoft)
            grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
            if np.isfinite(grms) and grms > 1e-13:
                grad = g
        else:
            K = int(max(8, min(Kmax, Kmax * (0.5 + 0.5 * progress))))
            K = min(K, L)
            A = _topk_indices(b, K)
            H = _build_H(p, b, A)
            H -= np.mean(H, axis=1, keepdims=True)
            rn = np.linalg.norm(H, axis=1)
            good = rn > 1e-13
            if int(np.count_nonzero(good)) >= max(2, K // 2):
                if not np.all(good):
                    H = H[good, :]
                G = H @ H.T
                G = 0.5 * (G + G.T)
                wv = _qp_solver(G, 55)
                g = H.T.dot(wv)
                g -= float(np.mean(g))
                grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
                if np.isfinite(grms) and grms > 1e-13:
                    if it % 100 == 99:
                        tau_frac = 0.12 + 0.10 * (1.0 - progress)
                        gs = _top_weight_gradient(p, b, bmax, max(1e-300, bmax * tau_frac), Ksoft)
                        gs_norm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gs * gs)))
                        if np.isfinite(gs_norm) and gs_norm > 1e-13:
                            g = 0.68 * g + 0.32 * gs
                            grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
                        if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms <= 1e-13:
                            grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
                    grad = g

        if grad is None:
            tau_frac = 0.10 + 0.16 * (1.0 - progress)
            g = _top_weight_gradient(p, b, bmax, max(1e-300, bmax * tau_frac), Ksoft)
            grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
            if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms <= 1e-13:
                v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n)
                v -= float(np.mean(v))
                v = np.clip(v, -35.0, 35.0)
                it += 1
                continue
            grad = g

        grad = grad / max(1e-300, grms)
        grad = np.clip(grad, -10.0, 10.0)

        t += 1
        mu = beta1 * mu + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        nu = beta2 * nu + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
        b1 = beta1 ** t
        b2 = beta2 ** t
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        lr = base_lr * (1.0 - 0.55 * progress)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        step = np.clip(step, -0.4, 0.4)
        v = v - lr * step
        v -= float(np.mean(v))
        v = np.clip(v, -35.0, 35.0)
        it += 1

    p = _soft(v)
    F = np.fft.fft(p, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    S = float(p.sum())
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()) / (S * S))
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best = p.copy()
    return _norm_seq(best), float(best_sc)


def _process_stage(n, deadline, prev_a, rng):
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= deadline:
        if prev_a is None:
            a = np.ones(int(max(3, n)), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a = _interpolate(prev_a, int(n))
        return a, _score_with_fft(a)

    n = int(max(3, n))
    best_a = None if prev_a is None else _interpolate(prev_a, n)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    best_sc = _score_with_fft(best_a)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    inits = _initial_candidates(n, rng, prev_a)
    scored = []
    for a in inits:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
            break
        aa = _norm_seq(a)
        sc = _score_with_fft(aa, m)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, aa))

    if not scored:
        scored.append((best_sc, best_a))

    if n <= 1024:
        base_k = 4
    elif n <= 4096:
        base_k = 3
    else:
        base_k = 2
    rem_init = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    while base_k > 1 and rem_init < 120.0 * base_k:
        base_k -= 1

    sel = _diverse_top(scored, base_k, 0.12)
    if not sel:
        sel = [scored[0]]

    ws = [0.56, 0.26, 0.12, 0.06]
    ws = ws[:max(1, len(sel))]
    wsum = float(sum(ws))
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        ws = np.ones(len(sel), dtype=np.float64)
        wsum = float(len(sel))

    for i, (sc, a) in enumerate(sel):
        now2 = time.monotonic()
        if now2 + 18.0 >= deadline:
            break
        rem = deadline - now2
        w = float(ws[i]) / wsum if i < len(ws) else 0.05
        alloc = rem * w
        alloc = max(20.0, alloc)
        alloc = min(alloc, 900.0, max(5.0, rem - 8.0))
        if alloc <= 5.0:
            break
        seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2 ** 31 - 1))
        aopt, scopt = _optimize_seq(a, n, alloc, seed)
        print("    n=%d cand%d score=%.6f best_stage=%.6f" % (n, i, scopt, best_sc))
        if np.isfinite(scopt) and scopt < best_sc:
            best_sc = scopt
            best_a = aopt

    return _norm_seq(best_a), float(best_sc)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + budget_s
    final_reserve = 180.0
    work_deadline = start + max(60.0, budget_s - final_reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    prev = None
    try:
        cand = height_sequence_1
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.ndim != 1:
            arr = arr.ravel()
        if arr.size > 0 and np.isfinite(arr).all():
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                prev = arr / float(arr.sum())
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    if prev is not None:
        best_a = _interpolate(prev, 2048)
        best_score = _score_with_fft(best_a)
        print("Interpolated previous length=%d score=%.6f" % (len(best_a), best_score))
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = _score_with_fft(best_a)
        print("No previous sequence; starting uniform score=%.6f" % best_score)

    base = max(60.0, work_deadline - start)
    stages = [
        (512, 0.02),
        (1024, 0.04),
        (2048, 0.14),
        (4096, 0.28),
        (8192, 0.38),
    ]

    cum = 0.0
    for n, frac in stages:
        cum += frac
        stage_dead = min(work_deadline, start + base * min(1.0, cum))
        if time.monotonic() + 20.0 >= stage_dead:
            break
        print("Stage n=%d stage_deadline=%.1fs current_best=%.6f" % (n, stage_dead, best_score))
        a_new, sc_new = _process_stage(int(n), stage_dead, best_a, rng)
        if a_new is not None and np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_a = a_new
            best_score = sc_new
        print("After stage n=%d best_score=%.6f" % (n, best_score))

    if time.monotonic() < work_deadline - 260.0 and best_a is not None:
        print("Extra stage n=16384 current_best=%.6f" % best_score)
        a_new, sc_new = _process_stage(16384, work_deadline, best_a, rng)
        if a_new is not None and np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_a = a_new
            best_score = sc_new
        print("After extra stage n=16384 best_score=%.6f" % best_score)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = _norm_seq(best_a)

    scale = 100.0
    final_list = [float(x * scale) for x in best_a]

    try:
        if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 220.0:
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
            print("Final FFT-like score=%.6f exact score=%.6f n=%d" % (best_score, ex, len(final_list)))
            if not np.isfinite(ex):
                best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
                final_list = [float(x * scale) for x in best_a]
    except Exception:
        pass

    return final_list
```
